Good. Now I have a complete understanding:

1. **context_window_mode** is handled in ctx_manager.py. Supported modes are: `"full"`, `"limited_multi_turn"`, `"single_turn"`. Worker 1 is adding `"step_independent"` mode.

2. **low_var_kl** is supported by verl's `core_algos.py` and is actually the default in verl's actor config. RAGEN's `dp_actor.py` uses it through `kl_penalty()`.


3. **use_invalid_action_penalty** and **invalid_action_penalty_coef** are verl-agent parameters that don't exist in RAGEN yet, so I need to check whether verl's dp_actor already supports them or if they're a new addition. The model checkpoint from recent experiments is at `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen3_exp021_ragen_sft_v4`, and the Hydra configuration is set up in `train.py` to load configs from the `/root/cost` directory.

4. All configs inherit from base.yaml using `defaults: - base`, and the training runs use `cd /root/cost-verify-agent-rl/RAGEN && python train.py --config-name &lt;name>` with optional parameter overrides. Let me check verl-agent's run_alfworld_exp025.sh to see how they handle this, and verify whether `use_invalid_action_penalty` is already implemented in verl's dp_actor.

Hydra 配置验证通过，所有关键参数解析正确。

**已创建的文件**：

| 文件 | 路径 |
|---|---|
| 配置 YAML | `RAGEN/config/_alfworld_exp026_step_independent_grpo.yaml` |
| 训练脚本 | `scripts/run_exp026_step_independent_grpo.sh` |
| Dry-run 脚本 | `scripts/run_exp026_dryrun.sh` |

**相比 exp022-24 的关键变更**：

| 参数 | exp022-24 | exp026 | 来源 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kl_loss_coef` | 0.5 | **0.01** | verl-agent default |
| `kl_loss_type` | kl | **low_var_kl** | verl default (k3) |
| `lr` | 5e-7 | **1e-6** | verl-agent default |
| `context_window_mode` | full | **step_independent** | Worker 1 新增 |
| `max_context_window` | -1 (全量) | **5** (bounded) | 配合 step-independent |
| `max_turn` | 30 | **50** | 对齐 verl-agent |
| `use_turn_scores` | False | **True** | Worker 2 step-level reward |
| `total_training_steps` | 30 | **150** | 充分训练 |
| `test_freq` | 20 | **5** | 更频繁验证 |
| `save_freq` | 5 | **25** | 减少 IO |
| `logger` | console only | **console+wandb** | 完整监控 |

**依赖 Worker 1/2 的部分**：
- `context_window_mode: "step_independent"` — 需要 Worker 1 在 ctx_manager.py 中实现
- `use_turn_scores: True` — 需要 Worker 2 的 step-level reward 计算
- `invalid_action_penalty` — RAGEN 尚未支持，已在 config 中标注 TODO

- `context_window_mode: "step_independent"` — 需要 Worker 1 在 ctx_manager.py 中实现
- `use_turn_scores: True` — 需要 Worker 2 的 step-level reward 计算
- `invalid_action_penalty` — RAGEN 尚未支持，已在 config 中标注 TODO
